#### Queries
##### Query 1
 -  Query identifier: `select bjs.*, j.jobAsJson   FROM (SELECT bj.id,              SUM(CASE WHEN (cj.parentJobId IS NOT NULL) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS totalChildCount,              SUM(CASE WHEN (cj.state = 'SUCCEEDED' OR cj.state = 'DELETED') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS succeededChildCount,              SUM(CASE WHEN (cj.state = 'FAILED') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS failedChildCount,              MAX(cj.updatedAt) as lastChildUpdatedAt         FROM jobrunr_jobs cj RIGHT JOIN jobrunr_jobs bj ON bj.id = cj.parentJobId         WHERE bj.isBatch = 1 AND bj.state  IN ('PROCESSED','FAILED')        GROUP BY bj.id) bjs,      jobrunr_jobs j   WHERE j.id = bjs.id and (bjs.lastChildUpdatedAt IS NULL OR bjs.lastChildUpdatedAt >= :lastChildUpdatedAfter OR bjs.totalChildCount = bjs.succeededChildCount)`
 - Query with values: `select bjs.*, j.jobAsJson   FROM (SELECT bj.id,              SUM(CASE WHEN (cj.parentJobId IS NOT NULL) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS totalChildCount,              SUM(CASE WHEN (cj.state = 'SUCCEEDED' OR cj.state = 'DELETED') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS succeededChildCount,              SUM(CASE WHEN (cj.state = 'FAILED') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS failedChildCount,              MAX(cj.updatedAt) as lastChildUpdatedAt         FROM jobrunr_jobs cj RIGHT JOIN jobrunr_jobs bj ON bj.id = cj.parentJobId         WHERE bj.isBatch = 1 AND bj.state  IN ('PROCESSED','FAILED')        GROUP BY bj.id) bjs,      jobrunr_jobs j   WHERE j.id = bjs.id and (bjs.lastChildUpdatedAt IS NULL OR bjs.lastChildUpdatedAt >= '2025-04-26T15:24:10.800+0200' OR bjs.totalChildCount = bjs.succeededChildCount)`
